# Test source

```ts
  1  | import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";
  2  | import { HomePage } from "../pages/HomePage";
  3  | import { SearchResultsPage } from "../pages/SearchResultsPage";
  4  | import { applyStealthScripts, humanDelay } from "../utils/stealthHelper";
  5  | import { TEST_DATA } from "../fixtures/testData";
  6  | 
  7  | test.describe("Amazon Search Flow", () => {
  8  |   test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  9  |     await applyStealthScripts(page);
  10 |   });
  11 | 
  12 |   test("TC01 - Should display results for a valid search term", async ({ page }) => {
  13 |     const home = new HomePage(page);
  14 |     const results = new SearchResultsPage(page);
  15 | 
  16 |     await home.navigate();
  17 |     await humanDelay(800, 1500);
  18 |     await home.searchFor(TEST_DATA.searchTerms.electronics);
  19 |     await results.waitForResults();
  20 | 
  21 |     const count = await results.getResultCount();
  22 |     expect(count).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  23 |   });
  24 | 
  25 |   test("TC02 - Should show results matching the search keyword", async ({ page }) => {
  26 |     const home = new HomePage(page);
  27 |     const results = new SearchResultsPage(page);
  28 | 
  29 |     await home.navigate();
  30 |     await humanDelay(500, 1200);
  31 |     await home.searchFor(TEST_DATA.searchTerms.simple);
  32 |     await results.waitForResults();
  33 | 
  34 |     const firstTitle = await page
  35 |       .locator('[data-component-type="s-search-result"] h2 span')
```
